  ┌──────────┬────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┐
  │ Priority │             Issue              │     Severity if Ignored     │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P0       │ API key restrictions on Google │ Financial ruin              │
  │          │  Cloud side                    │                             │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P0       │ Never expose API keys via IPC  │ Key theft → financial ruin  │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P0       │ Sanitize all text before       │ RCE chain                   │
  │          │ rendering in WebView           │                             │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P1       │ Rate limiting on translation   │ Cost amplification          │
  │          │ calls                          │                             │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P1       │ Audio circuit breaker for      │ Cost leak                   │
  │          │ silence                        │                             │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P1       │ Tauri v2 capability            │ Limits blast radius of      │
  │          │ permissions                    │ WebView compromise          │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P1       │ Parameterized queries + input  │ Data corruption, injection  │
  │          │ validation                     │                             │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P2       │ CSP headers                    │ Reduces XSS impact          │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P2       │ Minimal npm dependencies       │ Reduces supply chain        │
  │          │                                │ surface                     │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P2       │ Certificate pinning for gRPC   │ MITM in hostile networks    │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P3       │ WebView2 version check         │ Known CVE exploitation      │
  ├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┤
  │ P3       │ Sequence-ordered display       │ UX correctness, not         │
  │          │                                │ security                    │
  └──────────┴────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┘

  Build the P0 mitigations into Phase 1. P1 goes into Phase 2 (production
  hardening). P2/P3 before public distribution.

> Now, Let's Build UI/UX part. i want the UI/UX to be Zero Learning Curve to    
  the User. Example: Someone Opens the app and immediately knows what to do.    
                                                                                
  Here's what the user needs to accomplish:                                     
                                                                                
  1. Pick which app to capture audio from (Zoom, Chrome, etc.)                  
  2. Set source language and target language                                    
  3. Start/stop the translator                                                  
  4. Read the translated text in real-time                                      
                                                                                
  Design the UI for me.                                                         
                                                                                
  I want TWO modes:                                                             
                                                                                
  Mode 1: Control Panel (setup + full transcript view)                          
  Mode 2: Overlay (minimal subtitle bar floating over the meeting)              
                                                                                
  For each mode, give me:                                                       
                                                                                
  1. Exact layout (what's where, sizes, spacing)                                
  2. The components and their states (idle, listening, error)                   
  3. How the user switches between modes                                        
  4. How real-time text updates appear (animation? fade in? just append?)

  only ones currently outputting audio? A list of 50 processes is overwhelming. 
  How do I filter this to be useful?

  side, or just translation? What about showing the "interim" (still being      
  spoken)                                                                       
  text vs "final" (done) text? Should interim text look different (like greyed  
  out or italic)?

  (like YouTube captions)? How does the user reposition it? Can they resize it?

  dump them into the main UI with sensible defaults?                            
  9. Error states - how do I communicate "can't capture audio from this app" or 
  "ASR connection lost" without being annoying? The user is in a meeting, I     
  can't throw modal dialogs.                                                    
                                                                                
  Give me a mockup (ASCII) for both modes. Think of this like designing         
  captions for Netflix - minimal, non-intrusive, but always readable.

  Component Details

  1. Process Picker (The App Selector)

  Do NOT show all running processes. Show only apps currently holding an audio
  session.

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  🔊 Zoom Meeting                                         ▾  ⟳  │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
           │
           ▼ (clicked)
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                                                                 │
  │  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
  │  │ 🔊  Zoom Meeting                          zoom.exe      │   │
  │  │      ████████░░  (volume level indicator)                │   │
  │  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤   │
  │  │ 🔊  Google Chrome                         chrome.exe     │   │
  │  │      ███░░░░░░░  (volume level indicator)                │   │
  │  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤   │
  │  │ 🔇  Spotify                               spotify.exe   │   │
  │  │      ░░░░░░░░░░  (paused/silent)                        │   │
  │  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
  │                                                                 │
  │  ⟳ Not seeing your app? It must be playing audio to appear.    │
  │                                                                 │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  How it works:

  ┌──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │       Behavior       │                  Implementation                  │
  ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Only show apps with  │ Enumerate via IAudioSessionManager2 — only apps  │
  │ active audio         │ that have registered an audio session appear     │
  │ sessions             │                                                  │
  ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Live volume          │ Read peak meter from IAudioMeterInformation per  │
  │ indicator            │ session — shows which app is actually making     │
  │                      │ sound right now                                  │
  ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ App name + icon      │ Pull from process metadata (FileDescription from │
  │                      │  the exe's version info)                         │
  ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Auto-refresh         │ Poll every 2 seconds for new/removed audio       │
  │                      │ sessions                                         │
  ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ ⟳ button             │ Manual refresh + shows helper text               │
  ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Sort order           │ Active audio (non-silent) first, then            │
  │                      │ alphabetical                                     │
  └──────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Why this works for zero learning curve: User opens dropdown → sees only 2-3
  apps (the ones making sound) → picks the one they want. No scrolling through
  50 processes. The volume indicator confirms "yes, this is the app making sound
  right now."

  Edge case — Chrome with multiple tabs:

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ 🔊  Google Chrome                              chrome.exe       │
  │      ████░░░░░░                                                 │
  │      ℹ Captures all Chrome audio (all tabs)                     │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Explain this upfront in the dropdown. WASAPI process loopback captures all
  Chrome audio — you can't pick a specific tab from the desktop app. If the user
  is playing YouTube in another tab, it'll get captured too. Mention this so
  it's not a surprise.

  4. Real-Time Text Updates

  How text appears in the transcript panel:

  FINAL text (sentence complete, translated):
  ┌─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┐
  │  So the quarterly numbers   │  எனவே காலாண்டு எண்கள்         │
  │  are looking strong this    │  இந்த மாதம் வலுவாக            │
  │  month.                     │  இருக்கின்றன.                 │
  │                             │                               │
  │  We exceeded our target     │  நாம் இலக்கை 15               │  ← appears with
  │  by 15 percent.             │  சதவீதம் தாண்டினோம்.          │    fade-in (200ms)
  └─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┘

  INTERIM text (still being spoken, not yet translated):
  ┌─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┐
  │  I think we should          │                               │
  │  reinvest in the...         │         (waiting)             │
  │  ↑                          │                               │
  └─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┘
    Style: #888888 (grey), italic
    Updates in-place (replaces previous interim)
    No translation shown until sentence finalizes

  Animation behavior:

  ┌───────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┬──────────┐
  │         Event         │              Animation               │ Duration │
  ├───────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┤
  │ Interim text updating │ Replace in-place, no animation       │ Instant  │
  │                       │ (would be jittery at 300ms updates)  │          │
  ├───────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┤
  │ Interim → Final       │ Text color fades grey→white, style   │ 150ms    │
  │ (sentence complete)   │ italic→normal                        │          │
  ├───────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┤
  │ Translation appears   │ Fade-in on the right column          │ 200ms    │
  ├───────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┤
  │ New sentence pushes   │ Smooth scroll, scroll-behavior:      │ 200ms    │
  │ old ones up           │ smooth                               │          │
  ├───────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┤
  │ Auto-scroll to bottom │ Always follow latest unless user     │ —        │
  │                       │ scrolls up manually                  │          │
  └───────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────┴──────────┘

  Auto-scroll lock:

  User scrolls up to re-read → auto-scroll pauses
  New text arrives → show pill at bottom:

                      ┌─────────────────────┐
                      │  ▼ New messages (3)  │
                      └─────────────────────┘

  User clicks pill or scrolls to bottom → auto-scroll resumes

  ---
  5. Transcript Display: Source + Translation

  Show both, side by side. Here's why:

  - Source text lets the user verify accuracy ("did it hear correctly?")
  - Bilingual users can compare
  - If translation is wrong, they can read the original
  - It builds trust — the user sees the system working, not a black box

  But in overlay mode: translation-only by default (space-constrained). Toggle
  button ◁ switches:

  Mode A (default overlay):
  │  நாம் இலக்கை 15 சதவீதம் தாண்டினோம்.                             │

  Mode B (toggled, shows both):
  │  We exceeded our target by 15 percent.                           │
  │  நாம் இலக்கை 15 சதவீதம் தாண்டினோம்.                              │

  Interim vs Final visual distinction:

  /* Final text - fully confident */
  .transcript-final {
    color: #ffffff;
    font-style: normal;
    opacity: 1;
  }

  /* Interim text - still being spoken */
  .transcript-interim {
    color: #6b7280;       /* grey */
    font-style: italic;
    opacity: 0.7;
  }

  /* Translation text */
  .translation-final {
    color: #4ade80;       /* green - visually distinct from source */
    font-weight: 500;
  }

  The green translation vs white source gives instant visual parsing — your eye
  learns within 10 seconds which line to read.

  ---
  6. Switching Between Modes

  Control Panel → Overlay:

  Status bar in control panel:
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  ⚡ Latency: 1.1s  │  ⏱ Session: 23:41  │  ◆ Pop Out          │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                      ↑
                                                Click "Pop Out"

  - Control panel minimizes to system tray
  - Overlay window appears at last-used position (or bottom-center default)
  - Translator keeps running — no interruption

  Overlay → Control Panel:

  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ ● ═══════════════════════════════════════════════  ▤  ◁  ×      │
                                                      ↑
                                                Click ▤ (expand icon)

  - Overlay closes
  - Control panel restores from tray
  - Transcript history is preserved (nothing lost)

  Keyboard shortcut: Ctrl+Shift+O toggles between modes. Shown in tooltip on
  hover.

  ---
  7. Overlay Positioning & Resizing

  Default position: Bottom-center, 60px above the taskbar.

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                                                                 │
  │                     ZOOM MEETING WINDOW                          │
  │                                                                 │
  │                                                                 │
  │                                                                 │
  │                                                                 │
  │                                                                 │
  │  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
  │  │ ● ════════════════════════════════════════════  ▤  ◁  ×  │  │
  │  │  நாம் இலக்கை 15 சதவீதம் தாண்டினோம்.                      │  │
  │  └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
  │                                                                 │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ ▌ Start │ Taskbar...                                            │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Repositioning:

  ┌───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┐
  │              Action               │              Behavior              │
  ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Drag the top bar (═══)            │ Move overlay anywhere on screen    │
  ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Drag right/left edge              │ Resize width (400px–900px)         │
  ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Double-click top bar              │ Snap back to bottom-center default │
  ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Position persists across sessions │ Saved in preferences               │
  └───────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────┘

  No resize handle for height. Height is auto — expands to fit content (1-4
  lines), max 180px. This prevents the user from making it awkwardly tall or
  accidentally covering their meeting.

  Multi-monitor: Overlay stays on the monitor where the user positioned it. If
  that monitor disconnects, snaps to primary monitor bottom-center.

  ---

  with Google Cloud Console instructions.

  ---
  9. Error States — Non-Intrusive Meeting-Safe Errors

  Never use modal dialogs. The user is in a meeting — a modal that steals focus
  is unacceptable.

  Error display: inline status bar that changes color

  NORMAL (during translation):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  ⚡ 1.1s  │  ⏱ 23:41  │  ◆ Pop Out                             │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  Background: transparent

  WARNING (degraded but working):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  ⚠ High latency (3.2s) — may be network congestion             │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  Background: #f59e0b (amber) at 15% opacity
  Auto-dismisses when latency recovers

  ERROR (broken, auto-recovering):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  ● Reconnecting to speech service...  (attempt 2/5)             │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  Background: #ef4444 (red) at 15% opacity
  Shows retry count. If all retries fail:

  FATAL (needs user action):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  ✕ Cannot capture audio from Zoom.  [ Troubleshoot ]            │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  Background: #ef4444 at 15% opacity
  "Troubleshoot" opens a small inline panel (NOT a modal)

  Specific error scenarios:

  ┌────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────┐
  │       Error        │           User sees            │  Auto-recovery?  │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ ASR stream timeout │ Nothing — seamless reconnect   │ Yes, always      │
  │  (5 min limit)     │ in background                  │                  │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ ASR stream drops   │ "Reconnecting..." in status    │ Yes, 5 retries   │
  │ (network)          │ bar                            │                  │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Target app stops   │ "Zoom is silent" in status bar │ Resumes when     │
  │ audio              │  (grey, not alarming)          │ audio returns    │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Target app closes  │ "Zoom closed. Select another   │ No, needs user   │
  │                    │ app." — picker highlights      │ action           │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ API key            │ "API key error. Check          │ No               │
  │ invalid/expired    │ settings." + settings link     │                  │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Rate limited by    │ "Paused — too many requests.   │ Yes,             │
  │ Google             │ Resuming in 30s."              │ auto-backoff     │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
  │ Internet           │ "No internet. Translations     │ Resumes on       │
  │ disconnected       │ paused." — source transcript   │ reconnect        │
  │                    │ continues if audio is local    │                  │
  └────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────┘

  In overlay mode, errors are even more minimal:

  Normal overlay:
  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ ● ═══════════════════════════════════════════════  ▤  ◁  ×      │
  │  நாம் இலக்கை 15 சதவீதம் தாண்டினோம்.                              │
  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Error overlay (recording dot changes color):
  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ ⚠ ═══════════════════════════════════════════════  ▤  ◁  ×      │
  │  Reconnecting...                                                 │
  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    ↑ dot turns amber/red. Hover for details. That's it.

  The recording indicator dot is your entire error communication surface in
  overlay mode:
  - 🟢 Green pulse = working
  - 🟡 Amber steady = degraded (high latency, reconnecting)
  - 🔴 Red steady = broken (needs attention → expand to control panel)

  ---
  Color System

  Background:          #0f0f0f (app window)
  Surface:             #1a1a1a (cards, panels)
  Border:              #2a2a2a (subtle separation)
  Text primary:        #ffffff (source transcript, UI labels)
  Text secondary:      #6b7280 (hints, interim text)
  Translation text:    #4ade80 (green — the star of the show)
  Accent (interactive):#2563eb (blue — buttons, links)
  Success:             #16a34a (active/recording state)
  Warning:             #f59e0b (amber — degraded)
  Error:               #ef4444 (red — broken)

  Why dark theme only (default): The overlay sits on top of meeting apps. Dark +
  semi-transparent is legible over any background — light, dark, or video
  feeds. A light-themed overlay would clash with dark-mode Zoom and wash out
  over bright presentations.

  Offer a light theme toggle for the control panel (some users prefer it), but
  the overlay stays dark always.

✻ Worked for 2m 26s

> Okay, now give me phases to build this project with 2 hours. I want entire    
  project to be completed.                                                      

  Thought for 42s (ctrl+o to expand)

● 2-Hour Build Plan — Honest Reality Check

  The full architecture we designed (Tauri + Rust WASAPI + gRPC + overlay) 
  cannot be built in 2 hours. The Rust WASAPI module alone is 3-4 days.

  Here's what CAN deliver a fully working app in 2 hours — same core
  functionality, different tech choices optimized for speed:

  ---
  Tech Stack Swap (Production vs 2-Hour)

  ┌──────────────┬─────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┐
  │  Component   │   Production (weeks)    │          2-Hour Build          │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Framework    │ Tauri (Rust + WebView)  │ Python + CustomTkinter         │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Audio        │ WASAPI process loopback │ pyaudiowpatch WASAPI loopback  │
  │ capture      │  (per-app)              │ (system-wide)                  │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ ASR          │ Google Speech v2 gRPC   │ Deepgram WebSocket (Python SDK │
  │              │                         │  — 5 lines)                    │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Translation  │ Google Translate REST   │ deep-translator (no API key    │
  │              │                         │ for basic use)                 │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ UI           │ React in WebView +      │ CustomTkinter (native window,  │
  │              │ overlay                 │ modern look)                   │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Overlay      │ Transparent             │ Tkinter topmost +              │
  │              │ always-on-top + WDA     │ overrideredirect               │
  └──────────────┴─────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────┘

  What you lose: Per-process capture (gets all system audio), screen-share
  exclusion, production error handling, cross-platform packaging.

  What you keep: Real-time transcription, punctuation, silence detection,
  translation EN↔TA, clean UI, start/stop, overlay mode.

  ---
  Phase 2: Audio Capture (0:15 – 0:35)

  20 minutes. Capture system audio as PCM bytes.

  # audio_capture.py

  import pyaudiowpatch as pyaudio
  import numpy as np
  from threading import Thread, Event
  from queue import Queue

  class AudioCapture:
      def __init__(self):
          self.audio = pyaudio.PyAudio()
          self.stream = None
          self.running = Event()
          self.audio_queue = Queue()

      def list_audio_apps(self):
          """List apps currently outputting audio (WASAPI loopback devices)."""
          apps = []
          info = self.audio.get_host_api_info_by_type(pyaudio.paWASAPI)
          for i in range(info["deviceCount"]):
              device = self.audio.get_device_info_by_host_api_device_index(
                  info["index"], i
              )
              if device["maxInputChannels"] > 0 and device["isLoopbackDevice"]:
                  apps.append({
                      "index": device["index"],
                      "name": device["name"],
                      "rate": int(device["defaultSampleRate"]),
                  })
          return apps

      def start(self, device_index: int, sample_rate: int = 16000):
          """Start capturing audio from the specified loopback device."""
          self.running.set()

          device_info = self.audio.get_device_info_by_index(device_index)
          native_rate = int(device_info["defaultSampleRate"])
          channels = device_info["maxInputChannels"]

          def callback(in_data, frame_count, time_info, status):
              if self.running.is_set():
                  # Convert to 16kHz mono for ASR
                  audio_np = np.frombuffer(in_data, dtype=np.int16)
                  if channels > 1:
                      audio_np = audio_np[::channels]  # Take first channel
                  # Simple downsample if needed
                  if native_rate != 16000:
                      ratio = native_rate // 16000
                      audio_np = audio_np[::ratio]
                  self.audio_queue.put(audio_np.tobytes())
              return (None, pyaudio.paContinue)

          self.stream = self.audio.open(
              format=pyaudio.paInt16,
              channels=channels,
              rate=native_rate,
              input=True,
              input_device_index=device_index,
              frames_per_buffer=native_rate // 10,  # 100ms chunks
              stream_callback=callback,
          )
          self.stream.start_stream()

      def stop(self):
          """Stop capturing."""
          self.running.clear()
          if self.stream:
              self.stream.stop_stream()
              self.stream.close()
              self.stream = None

      def get_chunk(self, timeout=1.0):
          """Get next audio chunk (blocking with timeout)."""
          try:
              return self.audio_queue.get(timeout=timeout)
          except:
              return None

  Test at end of phase:
  cap = AudioCapture()
  print(cap.list_audio_apps())  # Should show your speakers/headphones as 
  loopback
  cap.start(device_index=X)
  chunk = cap.get_chunk()
  print(f"Got {len(chunk)} bytes")  # Should print ~3200 bytes (100ms of 16kHz 
  16-bit)
  cap.stop()

  Play any audio on your system and verify chunks are non-zero.

  ---
  Phase 3: ASR Streaming (0:35 – 0:55)

  20 minutes. Real-time speech-to-text with Deepgram.

  # asr_engine.py

  import asyncio
  import threading
  from deepgram import (
      DeepgramClient,
      LiveTranscriptionEvents,
      LiveOptions,
  )
  from dotenv import load_dotenv
  import os

  load_dotenv()

  class ASREngine:
      def __init__(self, language: str = "en-US", on_interim=None,
  on_final=None):
          self.language = language
          self.on_interim = on_interim  # callback(text: str)
          self.on_final = on_final      # callback(text: str)
          self.client = DeepgramClient(os.getenv("DEEPGRAM_API_KEY"))
          self.connection = None
          self.loop = None
          self._thread = None

      def start(self):
          """Start the ASR streaming connection in a background thread."""
          self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
          self._thread.start()

      def _run(self):
          self.loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
          asyncio.set_event_loop(self.loop)
          self.loop.run_until_complete(self._connect())

      async def _connect(self):
          self.connection = self.client.listen.asyncwebsocket.v("1")

          async def on_message(self_conn, result, **kwargs):
              transcript = result.channel.alternatives[0].transcript
              if not transcript:
                  return
              if result.is_final:
                  if self.on_final:
                      self.on_final(transcript)
              else:
                  if self.on_interim:
                      self.on_interim(transcript)

          async def on_error(self_conn, error, **kwargs):
              print(f"ASR Error: {error}")

          self.connection.on(LiveTranscriptionEvents.Transcript, on_message)
          self.connection.on(LiveTranscriptionEvents.Error, on_error)

          options = LiveOptions(
              model="nova-2",
              language=self.language,
              smart_format=True,       # Punctuation + formatting
              interim_results=True,    # Get partial results
              endpointing=300,         # 300ms silence = end of utterance
              vad_events=True,         # Voice activity detection
          )

          if await self.connection.start(options):
              # Keep connection alive
              while True:
                  await asyncio.sleep(0.1)

      def send_audio(self, chunk: bytes):
          """Send audio chunk to Deepgram (called from audio capture thread)."""
          if self.connection and self.loop:
              asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(
                  self.connection.send(chunk), self.loop
              )

      def stop(self):
          """Close the connection."""
          if self.connection and self.loop:
              asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(
                  self.connection.finish(), self.loop
              )

  Test at end of phase:
  def on_interim(text):
      print(f"[interim] {text}")

  def on_final(text):
      print(f"[FINAL] {text}")

  asr = ASREngine(language="en-US", on_interim=on_interim, on_final=on_final)
  asr.start()
  # Feed audio from capture
  cap = AudioCapture()
  cap.start(device_index=X)
  while True:
      chunk = cap.get_chunk()
      if chunk:
          asr.send_audio(chunk)

  Phase 4: Translation (0:55 – 1:10)

  15 minutes. Translate finalized sentences.

  # translator.py

  from deep_translator import GoogleTranslator
  from threading import Thread
  from queue import Queue

  class TranslationEngine:
      def __init__(self, source="en", target="ta", on_translated=None):
          self.source = source
          self.target = target
          self.on_translated = on_translated  # callback(source_text, 
  translated_text)
          self.queue = Queue()
          self._thread = Thread(target=self._worker, daemon=True)
          self._thread.start()

      def translate(self, text: str):
          """Queue text for translation."""
          self.queue.put(text)

      def _worker(self):
          """Background worker that processes translation requests 
  sequentially."""
          translator = GoogleTranslator(source=self.source, target=self.target)
          while True:
              text = self.queue.get()
              if text is None:
                  break
              try:
                  result = translator.translate(text)
                  if self.on_translated:
                      self.on_translated(text, result)
              except Exception as e:
                  print(f"Translation error: {e}")
                  if self.on_translated:
                      self.on_translated(text, f"[Translation failed: {e}]")

      def set_languages(self, source: str, target: str):
          """Change language pair on the fly."""
          self.source = source
          self.target = target

      def stop(self):
          self.queue.put(None)

  Test:
  def on_translated(src, tgt):
      print(f"{src}  →  {tgt}")

  t = TranslationEngine(source="en", target="ta", on_translated=on_translated)
  t.translate("Hello, how are you?")
  # Output: Hello, how are you?  →  வணக்கம், எப்படி இருக்கிறீர்கள்?

  Phase 5: Control Panel UI (1:10 – 1:35)

  # ui_panel.py

  import customtkinter as ctk
  from audio_capture import AudioCapture
  from asr_engine import ASREngine
  from translator import TranslationEngine
  import threading

  ctk.set_appearance_mode("dark")
  ctk.set_default_color_theme("blue")

  LANGUAGES = {
      "English": {"code": "en", "asr_code": "en-US"},
      "Tamil": {"code": "ta", "asr_code": "ta"},
      "Hindi": {"code": "hi", "asr_code": "hi"},
      "Spanish": {"code": "es", "asr_code": "es"},
      "French": {"code": "fr", "asr_code": "fr"},
  }

  class ControlPanel(ctk.CTk):
      def __init__(self):
          super().__init__()
          self.title("Speech Translator")
          self.geometry("720x600")
          self.minsize(520, 400)

          self.capture = AudioCapture()
          self.asr = None
          self.translator = None
          self.is_running = False
          self.audio_devices = []

          self._build_ui()
          self._refresh_devices()

      def _build_ui(self):
          # Main container
          self.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
          self.grid_rowconfigure(4, weight=1)

          # === App Picker ===
          picker_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self)
          picker_frame.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=20, pady=(20, 10),
  sticky="ew")
          picker_frame.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

          self.app_var = ctk.StringVar(value="Select an audio source...")
          self.app_dropdown = ctk.CTkComboBox(
              picker_frame,
              variable=self.app_var,
              values=["Loading..."],
              height=40,
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14),
              state="readonly",
          )
          self.app_dropdown.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=10, pady=10, sticky="ew")

          refresh_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
              picker_frame, text="⟳", width=40, command=self._refresh_devices
          )
          refresh_btn.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(0, 10), pady=10)

          # === Language Selectors ===
          lang_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self, fg_color="transparent")
          lang_frame.grid(row=1, column=0, padx=20, pady=5, sticky="ew")
          lang_frame.grid_columnconfigure((0, 2), weight=1)

          self.source_lang = ctk.CTkComboBox(
              lang_frame,
              values=list(LANGUAGES.keys()),
              height=38,
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=13),
          )
          self.source_lang.set("English")
          self.source_lang.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=5, sticky="ew")

          arrow_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
              lang_frame, text="  ───►  ", font=ctk.CTkFont(size=16)
          )
          arrow_label.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=10)

          self.target_lang = ctk.CTkComboBox(
              lang_frame,
              values=list(LANGUAGES.keys()),
              height=38,
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=13),
          )
          self.target_lang.set("Tamil")
          self.target_lang.grid(row=0, column=2, padx=5, sticky="ew")

          # === Start/Stop Button ===
          self.start_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
              self,
              text="●  Start Translating",
              height=50,
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=15, weight="bold"),
              command=self._toggle_translation,
          )
          self.start_btn.grid(row=2, column=0, padx=20, pady=15, sticky="ew")

          # === Separator ===
          sep = ctk.CTkFrame(self, height=2, fg_color="#2a2a2a")
          sep.grid(row=3, column=0, padx=20, pady=5, sticky="ew")

          # === Transcript Area ===
          transcript_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self)
          transcript_frame.grid(row=4, column=0, padx=20, pady=(10, 10),
  sticky="nsew")
          transcript_frame.grid_columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1)
          transcript_frame.grid_rowconfigure(1, weight=1)

          # Headers
          ctk.CTkLabel(
              transcript_frame, text="ORIGINAL",
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=11, weight="bold"), text_color="#6b7280"
          ).grid(row=0, column=0, padx=15, pady=(10, 5), sticky="w")

          ctk.CTkLabel(
              transcript_frame, text="TRANSLATION",
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=11, weight="bold"), text_color="#4ade80"
          ).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=15, pady=(10, 5), sticky="w")

          # Source transcript
          self.source_text = ctk.CTkTextbox(
              transcript_frame, font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14),
              wrap="word", fg_color="#111111"
          )
          self.source_text.grid(row=1, column=0, padx=(10, 5), pady=(0, 10),
  sticky="nsew")

          # Translation transcript
          self.target_text = ctk.CTkTextbox(
              transcript_frame, font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14),
              wrap="word", fg_color="#111111", text_color="#4ade80"
          )
          self.target_text.grid(row=1, column=1, padx=(5, 10), pady=(0, 10),
  sticky="nsew")

          # === Status Bar ===
          status_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self, height=36, fg_color="#111111")
          status_frame.grid(row=5, column=0, padx=20, pady=(0, 15), sticky="ew")

          self.status_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
              status_frame, text="Ready", font=ctk.CTkFont(size=12),
              text_color="#6b7280"
          )
          self.status_label.pack(side="left", padx=15)

          overlay_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
              status_frame, text="◆ Pop Out", width=100, height=28,
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=12), command=self._open_overlay
          )
          overlay_btn.pack(side="right", padx=15, pady=4)

      def _refresh_devices(self):
          self.audio_devices = self.capture.list_audio_apps()
          names = [d["name"] for d in self.audio_devices]
          self.app_dropdown.configure(values=names if names else ["No audio 
  sources found"])
          if names:
              self.app_var.set(names[0])

      def _toggle_translation(self):
          if not self.is_running:
              self._start()
          else:
              self._stop()

      def _start(self):
          # Find selected device
          selected = self.app_var.get()
          device = next((d for d in self.audio_devices if d["name"] ==
  selected), None)
          if not device:
              self.status_label.configure(text="⚠ Select an audio source first")
              return

          src_lang = LANGUAGES[self.source_lang.get()]
          tgt_lang = LANGUAGES[self.target_lang.get()]

          # Start translation engine
          self.translator = TranslationEngine(
              source=src_lang["code"],
              target=tgt_lang["code"],
              on_translated=self._on_translated,
          )

          # Start ASR
          self.asr = ASREngine(
              language=src_lang["asr_code"],
              on_interim=self._on_interim,
              on_final=self._on_final,
          )
          self.asr.start()

          # Start audio capture
          self.capture.start(device["index"], sample_rate=16000)

          # Start feeding audio to ASR
          self._feed_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._feed_audio,
  daemon=True)
          self._feed_thread.start()

          # Update UI
          self.is_running = True
          self.start_btn.configure(
              text="■  Stop", fg_color="#16a34a", hover_color="#15803d"
          )
          self.status_label.configure(text="⚡ Translating...")
          self.app_dropdown.configure(state="disabled")

      def _stop(self):
          self.is_running = False
          self.capture.stop()
          if self.asr:
              self.asr.stop()
          if self.translator:
              self.translator.stop()

          self.start_btn.configure(
              text="●  Start Translating",
              fg_color="#2563eb", hover_color="#1d4ed8"
          )
          self.status_label.configure(text="Stopped")
          self.app_dropdown.configure(state="readonly")

      def _feed_audio(self):
          while self.is_running:
              chunk = self.capture.get_chunk(timeout=0.5)
              if chunk and self.asr:
                  self.asr.send_audio(chunk)

      def _on_interim(self, text: str):
          """Called from ASR thread — update source text with interim result."""
          self.after(0, self._update_interim, text)

      def _on_final(self, text: str):
          """Called from ASR thread — finalize and send to translation."""
          self.after(0, self._update_final, text)
          if self.translator:
              self.translator.translate(text)

      def _on_translated(self, source: str, translated: str):
          """Called from translation thread."""
          self.after(0, self._update_translation, translated)

      def _update_interim(self, text: str):
          # Show interim in grey italic at the end of source text
          # (We'll just update a "current line" approach)
          pass  # Simplified: handled by _update_final

      def _update_final(self, text: str):
          self.source_text.insert("end", text + "\n\n")
          self.source_text.see("end")

      def _update_translation(self, text: str):
          self.target_text.insert("end", text + "\n\n")
          self.target_text.see("end")

      def _open_overlay(self):
          from ui_overlay import OverlayWindow
          self.overlay = OverlayWindow(self)
          self.withdraw()  # Hide control panel

  Phase 6: Overlay Mode (1:35 – 1:50)

  15 minutes. Floating subtitle bar.

  # ui_overlay.py

  import customtkinter as ctk

  class OverlayWindow(ctk.CTkToplevel):
      def __init__(self, parent):
          super().__init__(parent)
          self.parent = parent

          # Window properties — floating, always on top, semi-transparent
          self.title("")
          self.geometry("560x120+400+800")
          self.attributes("-topmost", True)
          self.attributes("-alpha", 0.92)
          self.overrideredirect(True)  # Remove title bar
          self.configure(fg_color="#1a1a1a")

          # Track dragging
          self._drag_x = 0
          self._drag_y = 0

          self._build_ui()

      def _build_ui(self):
          # Top bar (drag handle + controls)
          top_bar = ctk.CTkFrame(self, height=28, fg_color="#111111",
  corner_radius=0)
          top_bar.pack(fill="x")
          top_bar.pack_propagate(False)

          # Recording indicator
          self.rec_dot = ctk.CTkLabel(
              top_bar, text="●", text_color="#ef4444",
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14)
          )
          self.rec_dot.pack(side="left", padx=10)

          # Close button → back to control panel
          close_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
              top_bar, text="×", width=28, height=22,
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14),
              fg_color="transparent", hover_color="#333333",
              command=self._close_overlay,
          )
          close_btn.pack(side="right", padx=5)

          # Expand button → show control panel
          expand_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
              top_bar, text="▤", width=28, height=22,
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=12),
              fg_color="transparent", hover_color="#333333",
              command=self._expand,
          )
          expand_btn.pack(side="right", padx=2)

          # Drag binding on the top bar
          top_bar.bind("<Button-1>", self._start_drag)
          top_bar.bind("<B1-Motion>", self._on_drag)

          # Translation text area
          self.text_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
              self,
              text="Waiting for speech...",
              font=ctk.CTkFont(size=15),
              text_color="#4ade80",
              wraplength=520,
              justify="left",
              anchor="w",
          )
          self.text_label.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=15, pady=10)

      def update_text(self, source: str, translation: str):
          """Update overlay with latest translation."""
          display = f"{source}\n{translation}" if source else translation
          self.text_label.configure(text=display)

      def _start_drag(self, event):
          self._drag_x = event.x
          self._drag_y = event.y

      def _on_drag(self, event):
          x = self.winfo_x() + event.x - self._drag_x
          y = self.winfo_y() + event.y - self._drag_y
          self.geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")

      def _close_overlay(self):
          self.parent.deiconify()  # Show control panel again
          self.destroy()

      def _expand(self):
          self.parent.deiconify()
          self.destroy()

       1 """

● The LIBCLANG_PATH environment variable isn't set in your terminal. LLVM is
  installed but the compiler can't find libclang.dll. Run these commands in the
  same terminal:

  set LIBCLANG_PATH=C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin
  cargo tauri dev

  To fix permanently (so you don't have to set it every time), run this once in
  an elevated (Admin) PowerShell:

  [System.Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable("LIBCLANG_PATH", "C:\Program 
  Files\LLVM\bin", "User")

  Then restart your terminal and cargo tauri dev will work without the manual
  set command.

  ---
  If it still fails, verify the file actually exists:

  dir "C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin\libclang.dll"

  If that file doesn't exist, LLVM may have installed to a different path. Find
  it with:

  where /R "C:\Program Files" libclang.dll

  Then use whatever path it reports as the LIBCLANG_PATH value.

● Compiles clean. The fix:

  Problem: The old get_device_name was just parsing the device GUID string as a
  fallback (never actually reading the Windows property store).

  Fix: Now uses device.OpenPropertyStore(STGM_READ) →
  store.GetValue(&PKEY_Device_FriendlyName) → .to_string() which reads the
  actual friendly name like "Speakers (Realtek High Definition Audio)" from
  Windows.

  set LIBCLANG_PATH=C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin
  cargo tauri dev

  The dropdown should now show proper device names.

● Both compile. Rebuild your app:

  set LIBCLANG_PATH=C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin
  cargo tauri dev

  What changed:

  The dropdown now enumerates audio sessions (apps currently using audio) via
  IAudioSessionManager2 → IAudioSessionEnumerator, instead of listing output
  hardware endpoints. It:

  1. Gets the default render endpoint
  2. Queries IAudioSessionManager2 for all active audio sessions
  3. For each session, gets the process ID via
  IAudioSessionControl2::GetProcessId()
  4. Resolves the PID to a process name (e.g. zoom.exe, brave.exe) using
  CreateToolhelp32Snapshot
  5. Shows either the session's display name or the capitalized process name
  6. Marks apps actively producing sound with 🔊
  7. Sorts active apps first

  You'll now see entries like:
  - Zoom Meetings 🔊 — zoom.exe
  - Brave — brave.exe
  - Spotify 🔊 — spotify.exe

  Note: The actual audio capture still uses endpoint loopback (captures all
  system audio). True per-process isolation via ActivateAudioInterfaceAsync with
  PROCESS_LOOPBACK is the next step — but the UI now correctly shows which apps
  are producing sound.

● Compiles clean. The key fixes:

  1. Dedicated thread — Audio session enumeration now runs on its own thread
  with a fresh COM apartment, avoiding conflicts with Tauri's tokio runtime
  threads.
  2. COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED instead of COINIT_MULTITHREADED — Windows audio
  session APIs work more reliably in STA mode.
  3. Proper cleanup — CoUninitialize() called at the end.
  4. Better display names — Shows "AppName — process.exe" format, filters out
  @-prefixed resource strings that Windows sometimes returns as display names.

  Rebuild:

  set LIBCLANG_PATH=C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin
  cargo tauri dev

  You should now see entries like:
  - Brave — brave.exe 🔊
  - Zoom — zoom.exe 🔊
  - Spotify — spotify.exe

  If it still shows nothing, play audio first (any app making sound), then click
  the refresh button — sessions only appear after an app has started outputting
  audio at least once.

● Now I can see the issue. Your C# test proves the API works. Comparing with my
  Rust code, I see two bugs:

  1. IsSystemSoundsSession().is_ok() skips ALL sessions — In windows-rs 0.58,
  this returns HRESULT where both S_OK (is system sounds) and S_FALSE (is NOT
  system sounds) satisfy .is_ok() since both are success codes. So we skip
  everything.
  2. eConsole vs eMultimedia — Your C# uses Role.Multimedia, I used eConsole.

  The fix is simple: remove IsSystemSoundsSession() entirely (the pid == 0 check
  already filters system sounds), and match the role.

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

  function with the fixed version:

● Compiles clean. Here's what was wrong and what I fixed:

  Root cause: session2.IsSystemSoundsSession().is_ok() was true for ALL sessions
  — not just system sounds. In windows-rs 0.58, IsSystemSoundsSession() returns
  HRESULT where both S_OK (= is system sounds) and S_FALSE (= is NOT system
  sounds) are success codes, so .is_ok() returns true for both. Every app was 
  being skipped.

  Fixes applied:
  1. Removed IsSystemSoundsSession() entirely — just use pid == 0 to filter
  system sounds (exactly what your C# code does)
  2. Changed eConsole → eMultimedia — matches your working C# test
  3. Changed COM init to let _ = CoInitializeEx(...) — don't abort if COM is
  already initialized, just proceed
  4. Simplified name logic — removed the " — process.exe" suffix, cleaner
  display

  Rebuild:
  set LIBCLANG_PATH=C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin
  cargo tauri dev

  You should now see:
  - Zoom 🔊 — zoom.exe
  - Brave 🔊 — brave.exe
  - Zoom — zoom.exe (the inactive session)

  Size: ~1.6GB (Q5 quantized — fast on GPU, near-identical quality)

  ingface.co/ggerganov/whisper.cpp/resolve/main/ggml-large-v3-turbo.bin

  ┌──────────────────────────────┬─────────────────┬────────────────────┐
  │                              │    CUDA 12.6    │     CUDA 13.3      │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼─────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ Release                      │ Aug 2024        │ 2026 (very new)    │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼─────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ whisper.cpp tested against   │ ✓ Widely tested │ Mostly untested    │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼─────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ whisper-rs community support │ ✓ Stable        │ Unknown edge cases │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼─────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ RTX 4060 support             │ ✓ Full          │ ✓ Full             │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼─────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ VS 2022 MSVC 14.43           │ ✓ Compatible    │ ✓ Compatible       │
  └──────────────────────────────┴─────────────────┴────────────────────┘

  Both would work technically, but 12.6 is the version the entire whisper.cpp
  ecosystem is built and tested against. 13.3 is too new to have been validated
  with whisper-rs builds.

● The issue is npx tauri dev starts a new process that doesn't inherit the env
  vars you set in the terminal. The permanent fix is a .cargo/config.toml in the
  project — this makes every cargo/tauri command pick up the CUDA path
  automatically without needing to set anything.

  bat:

● Bash(cd "D:/Trilogy/Task_1/speech-translator/src-tauri" && export
      PATH="$PATH:/c/Users/ALLEN JOSEPH/.cargo/bin" && cargo clean 2>&1 | tail
      -2)
  ⎿       Removed 10955 files, 8.6GiB total

● Now from your terminal run:

  cd D:\Trilogy\Task_1\speech-translator
  dev.bat

  ---
  Why this happened: npx tauri dev spawns a fresh Node.js process. Environment
  variables you set with set in a terminal only live in that terminal session —
  child processes from other launchers (npm scripts, VS Code tasks, etc.) don't
  inherit them. The .cargo/config.toml [env] section is read by cargo itself
  before any build, so it works regardless of how you launch.

  Going forward: Always use dev.bat to run the project. It ensures CUDA 12.6 is
  on PATH before CUDA 11.8 every time, and you never need to manually set
  anything.

  The first build after cargo clean will take ~5 minutes (compiling all
  whisper.cpp CUDA kernels for RTX 4060). Subsequent builds are instant since
  the cache is valid.

  ---
  What changed and what you'll see now:

  Flow is now:
  Audio → Whisper → Transcribed event → UI shows text immediately
                         ↓
                   MyMemory translate → Translated event → UI updates
  translation

  In the transcript panel:
  - As soon as Whisper finishes a sentence → source text appears instantly with
  ⏳ Translating...
  - ~1-2 seconds later → ⏳ Translating... is replaced with the Tamil
  translation

  Translation: Now uses MyMemory (free, no API key, 500 req/day limit — enough
  for several hours of meetings).

  Debug: You will now immediately see if Whisper is transcribing correctly,
  completely independent of whether translation works. If you see source text
  appearing but translation stays as ⏳ Translating... — that's a translation
  issue. If nothing appears at all — that's an audio/ASR issue.

  What you'll see:

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  🔊  Zoom — zoom.exe                          ▾  ⟳  │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │  EN English  ⇄  TA Tamil                            │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │  ● Start Translating                                 │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓  Audio Input  │
  │  ∿∿∿∿∿∿∿___∿∿∿∿∿∿∿___∿∿∿                            │  ← waveform
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │  ORIGINAL           │  TRANSLATION                  │

  How to read the waveform:

  ┌─────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │       Visual        │                 Meaning                 │
  ├─────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Flat line           │ Silent / no audio — check app selection │
  ├─────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Small ripples       │ Background noise being captured         │
  ├─────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Large peaks (green) │ Active speech detected                  │
  ├─────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Amber/red peaks     │ Very loud audio                         │
  └─────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────┘

  If the waveform stays flat after starting translation with Zoom/Brave
  selected, the audio capture isn't working and you'll know immediately without
  waiting for Whisper to fail.
